   # pair (i,v): old term h_i(1-h_v)=1-h_v; new 0; Δ= -(1-h_v); index n-1 + (i-v)
   # pair (v,i): old h_v(0)=0; new h_v*1 = h_v; Δ= h_v; index n-1+(v-i)
For j similarly:
   # pair (j,v): old 0, new 1*(1-h_v); Δ=1-h_v (using old h_v). But if v=i, old h_i=1 new? Need handle simultaneous. Do special for pairs (i,j),(j,i) separately.
Better: Create `h2 = h.copy(); h2[i]=0; h2[j]=1`. But copy O(n) per move too slow. Instead compute old bits with h; new bits for i/j. For pairs involving i/j:
- pair (u=i, v=v): old = 1*(1-h[v]) if v!=j else (1-0=1); new = 0*(1-h2[v]) =0. Use h2[v] if v=i/j. Δ=-old.
- pair (u=v, v=i): old = h[v]*(0) =0; new = h2[v]*1. For v!=i,j: h2[v]=h[v]; Δ=h[v]. For v=j: h2[j]=1 -> Δ=1.
- pair (u=j, v=v): old=0; new=1*(1-h2[v]). For v!=i,j: h2[v]=h[v], Δ=1-h[v]; v=i: h2[i]=0, Δ=1.
- pair (u=v, v=j): old=h[v]*(1)=h[v]; new=h[v]*0=0. For v!=i,j, Δ=-h[v]; v=i: h2[i]=0? Wait old h_i=1, new h_i=0, but in pair old also? For pair (i,j): old term h_i*(1-h_j)=1, new h2_i*(1-h2_j)=0.   Δ = -(1 - h[v]) + h[v]  # for (i,v) and (v,i) combined? For (i,v): Δ_i_v = - (1-h[v]). For (v,i): Δ_v_i = + h[v]. They have different lag indices, so add separately, but can compute.
  c[n-1 + i - v] -= (1 - h[v])
  c[n-1 + v - i] += h[v]
  c[n-1 + j - v] += (1 - h[v])
  c[n-1 + v - j] -= h[v]

  # (i,j): old 1, new0 => c[n-1 + i-j] -=1

def full_c(h,n): return np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, 'full') * (2.0/n)

`c_delta(h, c, p, q, w, n)` returns c_new or mutates copy. To avoid duplicate code, write:
```
def apply_pair_update(h, c, p, q, w, n):
    if w ==0: return c
    cnew = c.copy()
    hp = h[p]; hq=h[q]
    v = np.arange(n)
    mask = (v != p) & (v != q)
    hv = h[mask]
    # indices arrays
    idx = n - 1 + (p - v[mask]); cnew[idx] -= w * (1 - hv)
    idx = n - 1 + (v[mask] - p); cnew[idx] += w * hv
    idx = n - 1 + (q - v[mask]); cnew[idx] += w * (1 - hv)
    idx = n - 1 + (v[mask] - q); cnew[idx] -= w * hv
    old_pq = hp*(1-hq); new_pq = (hp-w)*(1-hq-w); cnew[n-1 + p-q] += new_pq - old_pq
    old_qp = hq*(1-hp); new_qp = (hq+w)*(1-hp+w); cnew[n-1 + q-p] += new_qp - old_qp
    return cnew
```

Need candidate generation based on gradients. How compute top gradient fast:
```
def grad_for_lags(h, lags, n):
  g=np.zeros(n)
  for kk in lags:
    d = kk - (n-1)
    if d==0: g += 1 - 2*h
    elif d>0: g[:n-d] -= h[d:]; g[d:] += 1-h[:n-d]
    else: m=-d; g[:n-m] += 1-h[m:]; g[m:] -= h[:n-m]
  return g
```

```
def smooth_max_value_and_weights(c, alpha, top_k=64):
   if top_k >= len(c): idx=np.arange(len(c)); cc=c
   else: idx=np.argsort(c)[-top_k:]; cc=c[idx]
   m=cc.max(); shifted=alpha*(cc-m); if shifted.max()>500: ...; w=exp(shifted)/sum
   return m + logsumexp/alpha, w, idx
```

```
def evaluate_pair(h, c, p, q, n, wmin, wmax, n_samples=9):
    if wmax-wmin < 1e-12: return None
    # candidate ws
    ws = np.linspace(wmin,wmax,n_samples)
    # maybe add 0 if inside
    # Could use quadratic upper from top? simpler
    v = np.arange(n)
    mask = (v != p) & (v != q)
    vv = v[mask]
    hv = h[vv]
    idx_pv = n - 1 + (p - vv)
    idx_vp = n - 1 + (vv - p)
    idx_qv = n - 1 + (q - vv)
    idx_vq = n - 1 + (vv - q)
    a1 = 1-hv; a2=hv; a3=1-hv; a4=hv  # a1=a3
    hp=h[p]; hq=h[q]
    old0 = hp*(1-hp)+hq*(1-hq)
    base = c.copy()? We'll copy per w.
    bestw=None; bestval=inf
    for w in ws:
       cn = c.copy()
       cn[idx_pv] -= w*a1
       cn[idx_vp] += w*a2
       cn[idx_qv] += w*a3
       cn[idx_vq] -= w*a4
       new0 = (hp-w)*(1-hp-w)+(hq+w)*(1-hq+w)
       cn[n-1] += new0-old0
       cn[n-1+p-q] += (hp-w)*(1-hq-w) - hp*(1-hq)
       cn[n-1+q-p] += (hq+w)*(1-hp+w) - hq*(1-hp)
       val = cn.max()
       if val < bestval - 1e-15:
          bestval=val; bestw=w; bestc=cn
    # refine around bestw: local linspace(bestw +/- step)
    if bestw is not None:
       step=(wmax-wmin)/n_samples
       for w in np.linspace(bestw-step, bestw+step, 7) within:
         evaluate
    return bestw,bestval,bestc
```
This copies c for each w; for 9+7=16 copies. For 100 pairs, 1600 copies length 600 = ~1M float operations, okay. But Python loops 100*16 vector ops =1600, fine.

Then call minimize_scalar(`pair_objective_pre`, args=(c, idx1,idx2,idx3,idx4,a1,a2,hp,hq,old0,oldpq,oldqp,n), bounds=(wmin,wmax), method='bounded')). In function:
```
def pair_objective_pre(w, c, idx1,...):
  cn = c.copy()
  if w:
     cn[idx1] -= w*a1
     cn[idx2] += w*a2
     cn[idx3] += w*a1  # same a1
     cn[idx4] -= w*a2
  cn[n-1] += (hp-w)*(1-hp-w)+(hq+w)*(1-hq+w) - old0
  cn[idxp] += (hp-w)*(1-hq-w)-oldpq
  cn[idxq] += (hq+w)*(1-hp+w)-oldqp
  return float(cn.max())
```

Projection function:
```
def project_box_sum(a,n):
   target=n/2.0
   # bisection lambda
   lo = -1.0 - np.max(a)
   hi = 1.0 - np.min(a)
   for _ in range(60):
      mid=(lo+hi)*0.5
      s=np.sum(np.clip(a+mid,0,1))
      if s < target: lo=mid
      else: hi=mid
   x=np.clip(a+(lo+hi)*0.5,0,1)
   # small correction? already near. Could adjust by distributing diff to non-saturated.
   diff = target - x.sum()
   if abs(diff)>1e-12:
      # add diff to non-saturated? Use room.
```

```
def enforce_sum(h,n):
   h=np.clip(h,0,1)
   target=n/2
   for _ in range(50):
      diff=target-h.sum()
      if abs(diff)<1e-15: break
      if diff>0: room=1-h; indices room>1e-12; if none: break; h[idx]+=diff/len(idx) maybe but might saturate.
      else: indices h>1e-12; h[idx]+=diff/len(idx)
   h=np.clip(h,0,1)
   # final leave one saturated to adjust exact residual
   if abs(h.sum()-target)>1e-14:
      idx=np.argmax(1-h) if diff>0 else np.argmax(h)
      h[idx]=np.clip(h[idx]+(target-h.sum()),0,1)
   return h
```
Projection bisection may leave tiny saturations; do enforce.

def make_pairs(h,g,n,rng,max_candidates=100):
   res=[]
   # top high/low g
   order = np.argsort(g)
   low = order[:40]
   high = order[-40:]
   seen=set()
   for p in high:
      if h[p] <= 1e-8: continue
      for q in low:
         if p==q: continue
         hq=h[q]
         wmin = max(h[p]-1.0, -hq); wmax = min(h[p], 1.0-hq)
         if wmax - wmin < 1e-5: continue
         # first-order potential: if g[p] > g[q], positive direction width wmax; if g[p]<g[q], negative width -wmin
         if g[p] > g[q]:

def best_pair_move(h, c, p, q, n, maxiter=25, xatol=1e-7):
   hp=h[p]; hq=h[q]
   wmin = max(hp-1.0, -hq); wmax = min(hp, 1.0-hq)
   if wmax - wmin < 1e-6: return None
   v=np.arange(n); mask=(v!=p)&(v!=q); vv=v[mask]
   hv=h[vv]
   idx1 = n-1 + (p - vv)
   idx2 = n-1 + (vv - p)
   idx3 = n-1 + (q - vv)
   idx4 = n-1 + (vv - q)
   a1=1.0-hv; a2=hv
   old0=hp*(1-hp)+hq*(1-hq)
   oldpq=hp*(1-hq); oldqp=hq*(1-hp)
   idxp=n-1+p-q; idxq=n-1+q-p
   # Call minimize_scalar with a wrapper function top-level
   res = minimize_scalar(pair_objective_pre, args=(c, idx1, idx2, idx3, idx4, a1, a2, hp, hq, old0, oldpq, oldqp, idxp, idxq, wmin, wmax), bounds=(wmin,wmax), method='bounded', options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'xatol': xatol})
   w=res.x
   val=res.fun
   # due to numerical, maybe endpoints not exact; accept if val < c.max()-??
   # Apply
   hp_new=clip(hp-w,0,1); hq_new=clip(hq+w,0,1)
   h2=h.copy(); h2[p]=hp_new; h2[q]=hq_new
   c2=full_corr(h2,n)
   return h2,c2,float(c2.max())
```

Need `pair_objective_pre` use `w` clamped to [wmin,wmax] to avoid out-of-bounds from optimizer. It returns `1e100` if outside.
```
def pair_objective_pre(w, c, idx1, idx2, idx3, idx4, a1, a2, hp, hq, old0, oldpq, oldqp, idxp, idxq, wmin, wmax):
   if w < wmin - 1e-12 or w > wmax + 1e-12:
       return 1e100
   if w < wmin: w = wmin
   if w > wmax: w = wmax
   cn = c.copy()
   if w != 0.0:
      cn[idx1] -= w*a1
      cn[idx2] += w*a2
      cn[idx3] += w*a1
      cn[idx4] -= w*a2
   cn[n-1]? Need n known to update diagonal. Pass n or idx0. idxp and idxq passed; diagonal index `idx0 = 2*n-2`? Since full length 2n-1, middle index n-1. We can pass idx0 = n-1.
```

Pseudo pair_search:
```
def pair_search(h0, n, end_time, rng, cap=30, max_no_improve=50):
    h = project_box_sum(h0,n).copy()
    c = full_corr(h,n)
    cur = float(c.max())
    best_raw = cur
    best_h = h.copy()
    no_improve = 0
    scale=0.01
    while time.time() < end_time - 2:
        cur = float(c.max())
        if cur < best_raw - 1e-12:
            best_raw=cur; best_h=h.copy()
        top_idx, weights = top_weights(c, top_k=32)
        g = weighted_grad(h,n,top_idx,weights)
        pairs = make_pairs(h,g,n,rng,cap=3*cap)
        if len(pairs)==0: break? maybe perturb.
        improved=False
        for pair in pairs[:cap]:
            if time.time()>end_time-2: break
            p,q,wmin,wmax,score=pair
            res = minimize_scalar(...)
            val=res.fun
            # Maybe due to bounded includes current, val <=cur+? If val < cur - 1e-11:
            if val < cur - 1e-11:
                h2 = h.copy(); w=res.x; h2[p]=clip(h[p]-w); h2[q]=clip(h[q]+w)
                c2 = full_corr(h2,n)
                newcur = c2.max()
                if newcur < cur - 1e-12:
                    h=h2; c=c2; improved=True; no_improve=0; break
                else:
                    # val was lower but recompute not? maybe skip
                    pass

            no_improve +=1
            if no_improve > 20 and time.time() < end_time - 20:
                # perturb best_h

                h = project_box_sum(best_h + rng.normal(0,scale,n), n)
                c = full_corr(h,n)
                no_improve = 0
    # final polish from best_h with no perturb?
    h2 = best_h.copy()
    c2=full_corr(h2,n)
    # run one more pair_search without perturb? Could call recursively? Simpler: local finalize

`make_pairs` should cap top groups. Let's implement without lambdas:
```
def make_pairs(h,g,n,rng,cap=90):
    pairs=[]
    order=np.argsort(g)
    low=order[:30]
    high=order[-30:]
    # To avoid p/q saturated, check width.
    for p in high:
       p=int(p); hp=h[p]
       if hp <= 1e-7: continue
       for q in low:
          q=int(q)
          if p==q: continue
          hq=h[q]
          wmin = max(hp-1.0, -hq)
          wmax = min(hp, 1.0-hq)
          if wmax - wmin < 1e-5: continue
          if g[p] > g[q]:
              width = wmax
              score = (g[p]-g[q])*width
              # directed p should be high-g, q low. If h[p] maybe lower, negative width? Wait if hp<hq, positive wmax small? But if g[p]>g[q], first-order positive direction w>0; width = wmax. Good.
          else:
              width = -wmin
              score = (g[q]-g[p])*width
          if score <= 1e-10: continue
          pairs.append((p,q,wmin,wmax,score))
    # random
    act = np.where((h > 1e-5) & (h < 1-1e-5))[0]
    if act.size > 1:
       for _ in range(50):
          p=int(rng.choice(act)); q=int(rng.choice(act)); if p==q: continue
          hp=h[p]; hq=h[q]; wmin=...; width=...
          score = abs(g[p]-g[q])*max(-wmin,wmax)+1e-6
          pairs.append(...)
    if len(pairs)>cap:
       scores=np.empty(len(pairs)); for i... scores[i]=pairs[i][4]
       ord=np.argsort(scores)[::-1][:cap]
       pairs=[pairs[int(i)] for i in ord]
    return pairs
```
But if high/low includes same positions? low and high from argsort disjoint unless n<60. Fine.
Potential pair (p,q) may appear with p low q high? We only iterate p from high, q low. Symmetric enough.

`weighted_grad`:
```
def weighted_grad(h,n,idx,weights):
  g=np.zeros(n)
  for kk,w in zip(idx,weights):
    if w <= 1e-16: continue
    d = int(kk) - (n-1)
    if d==0: g += w*(1-2*h)
    elif d>0:
       g[:n-d] -= w*h[d:]
       g[d:] += w*(1-h[:n-d])
    else:
       m=-d; g[:n-m] += w*(1-h[m:]); g[m:] -= w*h[:n-m]
  return g
```
All weights sum1, raw gradient. Good.

def project_box_sum(a,n):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   target=n/2.0
   lo = max(0.0, target) - np.max(a)  # maybe lower enough? Actually if a+lo <=0 all? lo = -max(a)-1 safe.
   hi = 1.0 - np.min(a) + 1
   # better: lo = target/n - max(a)?? not.
   lo = -np.max(a) - 1.0
   hi = 1.0 - np.min(a) + 1.0
   for _ in range(60):
      mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
      s=np.sum(np.clip(a+mid,0.1? 0,1))
      if s < target: lo=mid
      else: hi=mid
   x=np.clip(a+0.5*(lo+hi),0,1)
   return enforce_sum(x,n)

def resample(h,n):
   if len(h)==n: return h.copy()
   x1=np.linspace(0,2,len(h)); x2=np.linspace(0,2,n)
   return project_box_sum(np.interp(x2,x1,h),n)

Let's implement `pair_objective_pre` carefully with `n` and idxs.
```
def pair_objective_pre(w, c, idx1, idx2, idx3, idx4, a1, a2, hp, hq, old0, oldpq, oldqp, idxp, idxq, idx0, wmin, wmax):
    if w < wmin - 1e-9 or w > wmax + 1e-9:
        return 1e100
    if w < wmin: w = wmin
    if w > wmax: w = wmax
    cn = c.copy()
    if w != 0.0:
        cn[idx1] -= w * a1
        cn[idx2] += w * a2
        cn[idx3] += w * a1
        cn[idx4] -= w * a2
    cn[idx0] += (hp - w) * (1.0 - (hp - w)) + (hq + w) * (1.0 - (hq + w)) - old0
    cn[idxp] += (hp - w) * (1.0 - (hq + w)) - oldpq
    cn[idxq] += (hq + w) * (1.0 - (hp - w)) - oldqp
    return float(cn.max())
```
Note idxp and idxq can be same as idx0 if p==q, but p≠q. Could idxp equal idx1 etc? no due excluded. Good.

    v=np.arange(n); mask=(v!=p)&(v!=q); vv=v[mask]
    if vv.size ==0:
        # handle length 2? still works with empty arrays?
    hv=h[vv]
    idx1=n-1+p-vv ...
    a1=1-hv; a2=hv
    old0=hp*(1-hp)+hq*(1-hq)
    oldpq=hp*(1-hq); oldqp=hq*(1-hp)
    args=(c, idx1,..., n-1,p,q? no need p q? idxp=n-1+p-q, idxq=n-1+q-p, idx0=n-1, wmin,wmax)
    res=minimize_scalar(pair_objective_pre, args=args, bounds=(wmin,wmax), method='bounded', options={'maxiter':25,'xatol':1e-7})
    if res is None ... return (res.x,res.fun)
```

Let's design `project_box_sum` without lambdas. Use `np.clip`.
```
def project_box_sum(a,n):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float).copy()
   target=0.5*n
   lo=-np.max(a)-1.0
   hi=1.0-np.min(a)+1.0
   for _ in range(60):
      mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
      s=np.sum(np.clip(a+mid,0.0,1.0))
      if s < target: lo=mid
      else: hi=mid
   res=np.clip(a+0.5*(lo+hi),0.0,1.0)
   return enforce_sum(res,n)
```
`enforce_sum`:
```
def enforce_sum(h,n):
   h=np.clip(h,0,1)
   target=0.5*n
   for _ in range(50):
      diff=target-h.sum()
      if abs(diff) < 1e-14: break
      if diff > 0:
         room=1.0-h
         idx=np.where(room > 1e-12)[0]
         if idx.size==0: break
         add=diff/idx.size
         # avoid saturating: cap
         # simply h[idx] += add; clip; if saturate loop
         h[idx] += add
      else:
         idx=np.where(h > 1e-12)[0]
         if idx.size==0: break
         h[idx] += diff/idx.size
      h=np.clip(h,0,1)
   # exact residual with one coordinate
   diff=target-h.sum()
   if abs(diff)>1e-15:
      if diff>0:
         idx=int(np.argmax(1.0-h)); h[idx]=min(1.0,h[idx]+diff)
      else: idx=int(np.argmax(h)); h[idx]=max(0.0,h[idx]+diff)
   return h
```
This may not exactly hit if one coordinate saturates but diff small. Good.

`smooth_obj`:
```
def smooth_obj(x,n,alpha,top_k):
    c=np.correlate(x,1.0-x,'full')  # raw
    cc=c
    if len(c)>top_k:
       idx=np.argsort(cc)[-top_k:]
       cc=cc[idx]
       m=cc.max()
    else: m=cc.max()
    sh=alpha*(cc-m)
    mx=sh.max()
    if mx>500:
       val=m + (mx + np.log(np.sum(np.exp(sh-mx))))/alpha
    else:
       val=m + np.log(np.sum(np.exp(sh)))/alpha
    return float(val)
```
`smooth_grad`:
`idx=np.argsort(c)[-top_k:]`; compute weights; return weighted_grad(c? actually weighted_grad(h,n,idx,weights)). Use raw.
SLSQP with raw objective. Scale alpha? Raw counts ~57, alpha=200 maybe. If alpha too small, objective approximate average not max; too large, numerics. Use 2000.

`slsqp_smooth`:
```
def slsqp_smooth(h,n,deadline,maxiter=800):
   bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
   cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':sum_con,'jac':sum_con_jac,'args':(n,)}]
   try:
      res=minimize(smooth_obj,h,args=(n,2000,128),method='SLSQP',bounds=bounds,constraints=cons,options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-14})
      x=project_box_sum(res.x,n)
      return x
   except: return h.copy()
```

Now, block start function without lambdas:
```
def make_block_start(n,rng,nblocks):
   lens_float = rng.dirichlet(np.ones(nblocks))
   lens = np.floor(n*lens_float).astype(int)
   rem = n - int(lens.sum())
   for i in range(rem): lens[i % len(lens)] +=1
   # remove zero lens
   keep = lens[lens>0]
   if keep.size==0: keep=np.ones(1); lens=keep
   h=np.zeros(n); pos=0; val=rng.randint(0,2)
   h_list? Use loop
   for L in lens:
       if L<=0: continue
       h[pos:pos+L]=val; pos+=L; val^=1
   if pos<n: h[pos:]=val
   # adjust exact count
   target=n//2
   cur=int(round(h.sum()))
   diff=cur-target
   if diff>0:
       ones=np.where(h==1.0)[0]
       # flip ones with smallest index? choose at block ends? random
       flip=ones[:diff] if diff <= ones.size else ones
       h[flip]=0.0
   elif diff<0:
       zeros=np.where(h==0.0)[0]
       flip=zeros[: -diff]
       h[flip]=1.0
   return h
```
If diff > ones size? shouldn't if total <=n/2? Could if start val pattern all ones? With alternation, ones count maybe near half, but if lens weird no. Fallback project.

Could also use nblocks from random.

def make_sine_start(n,rng):
   x=np.linspace(0,2,n)
   periods=rng.choice([1,2,3]) # but no lambda; np choice array? Use int
   phase=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
   amp=rng.uniform(0.5,1.0)
   h=0.5 + amp*np.sin(np.pi*periods*x + phase)
   # maybe add another cosine
   h += 0.2*rng.uniform(-1,1)*np.sin(2*np.pi*1.5*x + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
   h=np.clip(h,0,1)

```

Pseudo pair_search returns scaled C:
```
def pair_search(h0,n,end_time,rng,cap=None):
   if cap is None: cap = 24 if n>400 else 36
   h=project_box_sum(h0,n)
   c=full_corr(h,n)
   best_raw=float(c.max()); best_h=h.copy()
   no_improve=0
   scale=0.01
   while time.time() < end_time - 1:
      cur=float(c.max())
      if cur < best_raw:
         best_raw=cur; best_h=h.copy()
      top_idx,weights=top_weights(c,32)
      g=weighted_grad(h,n,top_idx,weights)
      pairs=make_pairs(h,g,n,rng,cap=3*cap)
      improved=False
      for tuple in pairs[:cap]:
         if time.time()>end_time-1: break
         p,q,wmin,wmax,score=tuple
         mv=best_pair_value(h,c,p,q,n)
         if mv is None: continue
         w,val=mv
         if val < cur - 1e-10:
            h2=h.copy(); h2[p]=clip(h[p]-w); h2[q]=clip(h[q]+w)
            # project? sum preserved but clip maybe if w boundary; enforce
            h2=enforce_sum(h2,n) # maybe cheap
            c2=full_corr(h2,n); newcur=float(c2.max())
            if newcur < cur - 1e-12:
               h=h2; c=c2; improved=True; no_improve=0; break
      if not improved:
         no_improve +=1
         if no_improve >= 25:
            # restart
            if time.time() < end_time - 30:
               scale = min(0.25, 0.008 + 0.004*no_improve)
               h=project_box_sum(best_h + rng.normal(0,scale,n),n)
               c=full_corr(h,n)
               no_improve=0
            else:
               break
   # final recompute best
   h=project_box_sum(best_h,n); c=full_corr(h,n)
   return h, float(c.max())*2.0/n

`make_pairs` details: Use `cap` meaning output cap. But in pair_search we pass `cap=3*cap`? We can make `make_pairs` generate max `cap` (final sorted cap). Then pair_search eval up to cap. Good.
```
def make_pairs(h,g,n,rng,cap=90):
    pairs=[]
    order=np.argsort(g)
    low=order[:30]
    high=order[-30:]
    for ii in range(len(high)):
       p=int(high[ii]); hp=h[p]
       if hp <= 1e-7: continue
       gp=g[p]
       for jj in range(len(low)):
          q=int(low[jj]); hq=h[q]
          if p==q: continue
          wmin = hp - 1.0
          tmp = -hq
          if tmp > wmin: wmin=tmp
          wmax = hp
          tmp = 1.0 - hq
          if tmp < wmax: wmax=tmp
          if wmax - wmin < 1e-6: continue
          if gp > g[q]:
             width = wmax
             score = (gp - g[q])*width
          else:
             width = -wmin
             score = (g[q] - gp)*width
          if score <= 1e-9: continue
          pairs.append((p,q,float(wmin),float(wmax),float(score)))
    act=np.where((h > 1e-5) & (h < 1-1e-5))[0]
    extra=30
    if act.size > 1 and extra > 0:
       while extra > 0 and len(pairs) < cap*3:
          p=int(rng.choice(act)); q=int(rng.choice(act))
          if p==q: extra-=1; continue
          hp=h[p]; hq=h[q]; compute width; if width>...
             score=abs(g[p]-g[q])*max(-wmin,wmax)+1e-8
             pairs.append(...)
          extra-=1
    if len(pairs)>cap:
       scores=np.empty(len(pairs))
       for i in range(len(pairs)): scores[i]=pairs[i][4]
       ord2=np.argsort(scores)[::-1][:cap]
       new=[]
       for i in ord2: new.append(pairs[int(i)])
       return new
    return pairs
```
Potential: high/low top 30 each, 900 pairs, cap 90. Sorting okay.

def optimize_start(h0,n,end_time,rng,use_slsqp=True):
   h,c_scaled=pair_search(h0,n,end_time - (90 if use_slsqp and n<=300 else 5), rng)
   if use_slsqp and n<=300 and time.time() < end_time - 30:
       h2=slsqp_smooth(h,n,maxiter=500)
       c2=full_corr(h2,n); v2=c5_from_corr(c2,n)
       if v2 < c_scaled: h,c_scaled = h2,v2
       # quick pair polish with remaining
       if time.time() < end_time - 3:
           h,c2=pair_search(h, end_time, rng, cap=10)
   return h,c_scaled
```

def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
 t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+budget_s-3
 rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
 best_h=None; best_c=inf
 parent=None
 try: parent=np.array(initial_h_values,float)
 except: parent=None
 if parent is not None:
    n_par=len(parent)
    # stage 1 parent: allocate 45% maybe
    rem=deadline-t0
    end1=t0 + min(500, rem*0.45)
    h,c=pair_search(parent,n_par,end1,rng)
    if c<best_c: best=...
    # if binary rounded start, allocate 100s
    if time.time() < deadline - 120:
       hb=round_to_binary(parent,n_par)
       end2=time.time()+min(100,(deadline-time.time())*0.15)
       h,c=pair_search(hb,n_par,end2,rng)
       update
    # other n starts from best: 200,450,600? allocate based on rem
    other_ns=[200,450,600]
    for nt in other_ns:
       if time.time() > deadline-40: break
       # skip if nt == n_par? len best may be n_par but maybe best n changed. If nt==len(best_h): maybe do another start with block? We'll just continue if time.
       # start: resample best if best exists else parent
       if best_h is not None:
          hstart=resample(best_h,nt)
       else:
          hstart=project_box_sum(np.full(nt,0.5),nt)
       # allocate: if nt <=300:100, nt=450:150, nt=600:150
       alloc = 100 if nt<=300 else 180
       endn=time.time()+min(alloc,(deadline-time.time())*0.25)
       h,c=pair_search(hstart,nt,endn,rng)
       if c<best_c: update
       # optionally block start at this n if time
    # Additional block starts at n_par if time
    if time.time() < deadline - 100:
       for nb in [6,10]:
          hstart=make_block_start(n_par,rng,nb)
          endb=time.time()+min(60,(deadline-time.time())*0.2)
          h,c=pair_search(hstart,n_par,endb,rng)
          update
       hstart=make_sine_start(n_par,rng); ...
 else:
    n=300
    # starts: constant, block, round
    h0=project_box_sum(np.full(n,0.5),n); end=t0+min(300,rem*0.3); pair_search...
    ...
 # final SLSQP for best if n<=300 and time>70
 if best_h is not None and len(best_h)<=300 and time.time() < deadline - 60:
    h2=slsqp_smooth(best_h,len(best_h),maxiter=400)
    v2=compute_c5(h2)
    if v2<best_c: best_h,best_c=h2,v2
 # final short pair polish
 if best_h is not None and time.time() < deadline - 2:
    n=len(best_h)
    h2,c2=pair_search(best_h,n,deadline-1,rng,cap=10)
    if c2<best_c: best=...
    else: use best
 best_h=project_box_sum(best_h,n)
 best_c=compute_c5(best_h)
 return best_h,best_c,n
```

Use alpha 40 maybe.
```
def top_weights(c,top_k):
   idx=np.argsort(c)[-top_k:]
   cc=c[idx]
   m=cc.max()
   sh=40.0*(cc-m)
   sh=sh-sh.max()
   w=np.exp(sh)
   s=w.sum()
   if s==0: w=np.ones_like(w)/w.size
   else: w=w/s
   return idx,w
```
Since sh all <=0, no overflow. top_k=32. Good.

Draft:
```
import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize, minimize_scalar

def full_corr(x, n):
    return np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode='full')

def c5_raw(c, n):
    return float(np.max(c)) * (2.0 / n)

def compute_c5(h):
    n = len(h)
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')) * (2.0 / n))

def enforce_sum(h, n):
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = 0.5 * n
    for _ in range(40):
        diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            idx = np.where(1.0 - h > 1e-12)[0]
            if idx.size == 0:
                break
            h[idx] += diff / idx.size
        else:
            idx = np.where(h > 1e-12)[0]
            if idx.size == 0:
                break
            h[idx] += diff / idx.size
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(diff) > 1e-15:
        if diff > 0:
            i = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
            h[i] = min(1.0, h[i] + diff)
        else:
            i = int(np.argmax(h))
            h[i] = max(0.0, h[i] + diff)
    return h

def project_box_sum(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float).copy()
    target = 0.5 * n
    lo = -float(np.max(a)) - 1.0
    hi = 1.0 - float(np.min(a)) + 1.0
    for _ in range(60):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = float(np.sum(np.clip(a + mid, 0.0, 1.0)))
        if s < target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    x = np.clip(a + 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    return enforce_sum(x, n)
```
Bisection 60 might be overkill; okay.

`round_to_binary`:
```
def round_to_binary(h,n):
    out=np.zeros(n,float)
    if n%2==0:
       target=n//2
       idx=np.argsort(h)[::-1][:target]
       out[idx]=1
    else:
       target=n//2
       idx=np.argsort(h)[::-1][:target]
       out[idx]=1
       # set middle? to make sum .5
       rem=0.5
       if rem>0:
          i=int(np.argmin(np.where(out==1, -abs(h-1),?))) no; just project
       out=np.clip(out,0,1)
    return project_box_sum(out,n)
```
For even only. If odd, project will make fractional. Fine. We only use even.

`resample`:
```
def resample(h,n):
    if len(h)==n: return project_box_sum(h,n)
    x1=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,len(h))
    x2=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n)
    return project_box_sum(np.interp(x2,x1,h),n)
```

`top_weights`:
```
def top_weights(c, top_k):
    if len(c) > top_k:
        idx = np.argsort(c)[-top_k:]
    else:
        idx = np.arange(len(c))
    cc = c[idx]
    m = float(cc.max())
    sh = 40.0 * (cc - m)
    sh = sh - float(sh.max())
    w = np.exp(sh)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 0:
        w = np.ones_like(w) / w.size
    else:
        w = w / s
    return idx, w
```
`sh.max` is 0; good.

`grad_from_idx`:
```
def grad_from_idx(h,n,idx,weights):
    g=np.zeros(n,float)
    for ii in range(idx.size):
        w=weights[ii]
        if w <= 1e-15: continue
        k=int(idx[ii]); d=k-(n-1)
        if d==0:
           g += w*(1.0 - 2.0*h)
        elif d>0:
           m=d
           g[:n-m] -= w*h[m:]
           g[m:] += w*(1.0 - h[:n-m])
        else:
           m=-d
           g[:n-m] += w*(1.0 - h[m:])
           g[m:] -= w*h[:n-m]
    return g
```

`pair_objective_pre`: as above. Need include idx0.
```
def pair_objective_pre(w, c, idx1, idx2, idx3, idx4, a1, a2, hp, hq, old0, oldpq, oldqp, idxp, idxq, idx0, wmin, wmax):
    if w < wmin - 1e-10:
        return 1e100
    if w > wmax + 1e-10:
        return 1e100
    if w < wmin: w = wmin
    if w > wmax: w = wmax
    cn = c.copy()
    if w != 0.0:
        cn[idx1] -= w * a1
        cn[idx2] += w * a2
        cn[idx3] += w * a1
        cn[idx4] -= w * a2
    pn = hp - w
    qn = hq + w
    cn[idx0] += pn*(1.0-pn) + qn*(1.0-qn) - old0
    cn[idxp] += pn*(1.0-qn) - oldpq
    cn[idxq] += qn*(1.0-pn) - oldqp
    m = float(np.max(cn))
    if not np.isfinite(m): return 1e100
    return m
```
If w=0, diagonal update new-old 0; we skip? It still updates if code runs; okay. Could if w==0 return c.max() to save. Add:
```
if w == 0.0:
   return float(np.max(c))
```
But scalar may call at 0 often; save copy. However if we skip, no cn; good.
```
if abs(w) < 1e-15: return float(np.max(c))
```

`best_pair_value`:
```
def best_pair_value(h,c,p,q,n,maxiter=22):
    hp=h[p]; hq=h[q]
    wmin = max(hp-1.0, -hq)
    wmax = min(hp, 1.0-hq)
    if wmax - wmin < 1e-7:
        return None
    v=np.arange(n)
    mask=(v!=p)&(v!=q)
    vv=v[mask]
    hv=h[vv]
    idx1=n-1 + (p - vv)
    idx2=n-1 + (vv - p)
    idx3=n-1 + (q - vv)
    idx4=n-1 + (vv - q)
    a1=1.0 - hv
    a2=hv
    old0=hp*(1.0-hp)+hq*(1.0-hq)
    oldpq=hp*(1.0-hq)
    oldqp=hq*(1.0-hp)
    idxp=n-1 + p - q
    idxq=n-1 + q - p
    idx0=n-1
    args=(c, idx1, idx2, idx3, idx4, a1, a2, hp, hq, old0, oldpq, oldqp, idxp, idxq, idx0, wmin, wmax)
    try:
        res = minimize_scalar(pair_objective_pre, args=args, bounds=(wmin,wmax), method='bounded', options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'xatol': 1e-6})
    except Exception:
        return None
    if res is None or not np.isfinite(res.fun):
        return None
    return float(res.x), float(res.fun)
```
If `vv` empty, idx arrays empty, a empty; operations okay. `c` could be int? float.

`pair_search`:
```
def pair_search(h0,n,end_time,rng,cap=None):
    if cap is None:
        if n <= 200: cap=48
        elif n <= 350: cap=36
        else: cap=20
    h=project_box_sum(h0,n)
    c=full_corr(h,n)
    cur=float(c.max())
    best_raw=cur
    best_h=h.copy()
    no_improve=0
    scale=0.008
    while time.time() < end_time - 1.0:
        cur=float(c.max())
        if cur < best_raw:
            best_raw=cur; best_h=h.copy()
        idx,weights=top_weights(c,32)
        g=grad_from_idx(h,n,idx,weights)
        pairs=make_pairs(h,g,n,rng,cap=max(30, cap*2))
        improved=False
        for pair in pairs:
            if time.time() > end_time - 1.5:
                break
            p,q,wmin,wmax,score=pair
            mv=best_pair_value(h,c,int(p),int(q),n)
            if mv is None: continue
            w,val=mv
            if val < cur - 1e-9:
                h2=h.copy()
                pn=np.clip(h[p]-w,0.0,1.0)
                qn=np.clip(h[q]+w,0.0,1.0)
                h2[p]=pn; h2[q]=qn
                h2=np.clip(h2,0.0,1.0)
                sdiff=0.5*n - float(h2.sum())
                if abs(sdiff)>1e-13:
                   h2=enforce_sum(h2,n)
                c2=full_corr(h2,n)
                newcur=float(c2.max())
                if newcur < cur - 1e-12:
                    h=h2; c=c2; improved=True; no_improve=0; break
        if not improved:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve >= 25:
                if time.time() < end_time - 30:
                    scale = min(0.25, 0.006 + 0.0035 * float(no_improve))
                    h2 = best_h + rng.normal(0.0, scale, n)
                    h = project_box_sum(h2, n)
                    c = full_corr(h,n)
                    no_improve = 0
                else:
                    break
    h=project_box_sum(best_h,n)
    c=full_corr(h,n)
    return h, float(c.max())*(2.0/n)
```
Potential: If best_h is poor and current h after perturb is much worse, best_raw remains. If no_improve repeatedly from perturb, it will keep perturbing best. Good.

The loop doesn't break, but `improved` false; it will evaluate other pairs. Good. But if val lower but recompute fails due to tiny clipping, maybe that candidate rejected; could still later. Good.

Should we break after first accepted to recompute gradients. Yes.

Now, `make_block_start` code. Need `rng.randint(0,2)`. `lens` length maybe zero if nblocks and dirichlet; floor could zero. Remove zeros.
```
def make_block_start(n,rng,nblocks):
    nblocks=int(nblocks)
    wf=rng.dirichlet(np.ones(nblocks))
    lens=np.floor(n*wf).astype(int)
    rem=n-int(lens.sum())
    for i in range(rem):
       lens[i % len(lens)] += 1
    lens=lens[lens>0]
    if lens.size==0:
       lens=np.ones(1,int)
    h=np.zeros(n,float)
    pos=0
    val=float(rng.randint(0,2))
    for L in lens:
       L=int(L)
       if L<=0: continue
       if pos+L>n: L=n-pos
       h[pos:pos+L]=val
       pos += L
       val = 1.0 - val
       if pos>=n: break
    if pos<n: h[pos:]=val
    target=n//2
    cur=int(round(float(h.sum())))
    diff=cur-target
    if diff>0:
       ones=np.where(h>0.5)[0]
       if diff <= ones.size:
          h[ones[:diff]]=0.0
       else:
          h[ones]=0.0
    elif diff<0:
       zeros=np.where(h<=0.5)[0]
       nd=-diff
       if nd <= zeros.size: h[zeros[:nd]]=1.0
       else: h[zeros]=1.0
    return project_box_sum(h,n)
```
Project ensures sum exact. Flipping first diff may create small blocks at beginning; random enough. Could randomize flip order: `idx = rng.choice(ones, size=min(diff,ones.size), replace=False)`. Use for diversity. But if diff > size, all. Use.
```
if diff>0:
   k=min(diff, ones.size); if k>0: h[rng.choice(ones,k,replace=False)]=0
```
Need if diff not int? target n//2 for even; if odd, h sum int, diff. okay.

`make_sine_start`:
```
def make_sine_start(n,rng):
    x=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n)
    f=rng.randint(1,4)
    ph=rng.uniform(0.0,6.283185307179586)
    amp=rng.uniform(0.5,1.0)
    h=0.5 + amp*np.sin(np.pi*f*x + ph)
    h += 0.25*rng.normal(0.0,0.2,n)
    h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    return project_box_sum(h,n)
```
Maybe too noisy. Fine.

`sum_con`, etc.
```
def sum_con(x,n):
    return float(np.sum(x) - 0.5*n)
def sum_con_jac(x,n):
    return np.ones(len(x), dtype=float)
def smooth_obj(x,n,alpha,top_k):
    c=full_corr(x,n)
    if len(c)>top_k:
       idx=np.argsort(c)[-top_k:]
       cc=c[idx]
    else: cc=c
    m=float(cc.max())
    sh=alpha*(cc-m)
    sh=sh - float(sh.max())
    val=m + float(np.log(np.sum(np.exp(sh))))/alpha
    return val
def smooth_grad(x,n,alpha,top_k):
    c=full_corr(x,n)
    idx,w=top_weights(c,top_k)
    return grad_from_idx(x,n,idx,w)
def slsqp_smooth(h,n,maxiter=500):
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*len(h)
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':sum_con,'jac':sum_con_jac,'args':(n,)}]
    try:
       res=minimize(smooth_obj,h,jac=smooth_grad,args=(n,2000,128),method='SLSQP',bounds=bounds,constraints=cons,options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-14})
       return project_box_sum(res.x,n)
    except Exception:
       return h.copy()
```
If SLSQP fails and res.x None? project handles None? res.x maybe None; catch? Add if res.x is None return h.
```
if res.x is None: return h.copy()
```

def project_box_sum(a,n):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); target=n/2
    lo=-a.max()-1; hi=1-a.min()+1
    for _ in range(50):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2; s=np.sum(np.clip(a+mid,0,1))
       if s<target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    return np.clip(a+(lo+hi)*0.5,0,1)
```

```
def project_box_sum(a,n):
    a=np.clip(np.asarray(a,float),0,1)
    target=0.5*n
    lo=-float(a.max())-1.0
    hi=1.0-float(a.min())+1.0
    for _ in range(55):
       mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
       if np.sum(np.clip(a+mid,0,1)) < target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    x=np.clip(a+0.5*(lo+hi),0,1)
    diff=target-float(x.sum())
    if abs(diff)>1e-13:
       idx=np.where((x>1e-12)&(x<1-1e-12))[0]
       if idx.size:
          x[idx]+=diff/idx.size
       else:
          i=int(np.argmax(1-x)) if diff>0 else int(np.argmax(x))
          x[i]+=diff
       x=np.clip(x,0,1)
    return x
```
If no interior and diff too large, adjust one may saturate but diff small. Good.

`binary_from`:
```
def binary_from(h):
    h=np.asarray(h,float); n=len(h); out=np.zeros(n)
    idx=np.argsort(h)[::-1]; k=n//2
    out[idx[:k]]=1.0
    return project_box_sum(out,n)
```

`resample`:
```
def resample(h,n):
    if len(h)==n: return project_box_sum(h,n)
    x1=np.linspace(0,2,len(h)); x2=np.linspace(0,2,n)
    return project_box_sum(np.interp(x2,x1,h),n)
```

`block_start` compact:
```
def block_start(n,rng,nblocks):
    wf=rng.dirichlet(np.ones(nblocks)); lens=np.floor(n*wf).astype(int)
    rem=n-int(lens.sum())
    for i in range(rem): lens[i%len(lens)] += 1
    lens=lens[lens>0]
    h=np.zeros(n); pos=0; val=float(rng.randint(0,2))
    for L in lens:
       L=int(L)
       if L<=0 or pos>=n: continue
       if pos+L>n: L=n-pos
       h[pos:pos+L]=val; pos+=L; val=1-val
    if pos<n: h[pos:]=val
    target=n//2; cur=int(round(h.sum())); diff=cur-target
    if diff>0:
       ones=np.where(h>0.5)[0]; k=min(diff,ones.size)
       if k: h[rng.choice(ones,k,replace=False)]=0
    elif diff<0:
       zeros=np.where(h<=0.5)[0]; k=min(-diff, zeros.size)
       if k: h[rng.choice(zeros,k,replace=False)]=1
    return project_box_sum(h,n)
```
Good.

`pair_search` compact. Need handle if pairs empty: no_improve.
```
def pair_search(h0,n,end_time,rng,cap=None):
    if cap is None: cap=48 if n<=200 else (36 if n<=350 else 20)
    h=project_box_sum(h0,n); c=full_corr(h,n); cur=float(c.max()); best=h.copy(); best_raw=cur; no=0
    while time.time()<end_time-1:
       cur=float(c.max())
       if cur<best_raw: best_raw=cur; best=h.copy()
       idx,w=top_weights(c,32); g=grad_from_idx(h,n,idx,w)
       pairs=make_pairs(h,g,n,rng,max(30,cap*2))
       improved=False
       for p,q,wmin,wmax,sc in pairs:
          if time.time()>end_time-1.5: break
          mv=best_pair_value(h,c,p,q,n)
          if mv is None: continue
          ww,val=mv
          if val<cur-1e-9:
             h2=h.copy(); h2[p]=np.clip(h[p]-ww,0,1); h2[q]=np.clip(h[q]+ww,0,1)
             h2=project_box_sum(h2,n)
             c2=full_corr(h2,n); nc=float(c2.max())
             if nc<cur-1e-12:
                h=h2; c=c2; improved=True; no=0; break
       if not improved:
          no+=1
          if no>=25:
             if time.time()<end_time-30:
                scale=min(0.25,0.006+0.0035*no)
                h=project_box_sum(best+rng.normal(0,scale,n),n)
                c=full_corr(h,n); no=0
             else: break
    return best, float(full_corr(best,n).max())*2.0/n
```
Note calling project_box_sum on h2 may slightly alter other coordinates to fix sum, which can undo move if diff tiny? diff near 1e-16, adjust on interior maybe tiny. Good. But full project 55*600=33k per accept; if 1000 accepts 33M, okay. Use project for safety.

Could if no improvements, no grows; scale formula uses no after reset? okay.

`make_pairs` compact. Need ensure returns list p,q ints. Use `cap` final cap.
```
def make_pairs(h,g,n,rng,cap):
    P=[]; order=np.argsort(g); low=order[:30]; high=order[-30:]
    for p in high:
       p=int(p); hp=h[p]
       if hp<=1e-7: continue
       for q in low:
          q=int(q)
          if p==q: continue
          hq=h[q]; wmin=hp-1.0; t=-hq
          if t>wmin: wmin=t
          wmax=hp; t=1-hq
          if t<wmax: wmax=t
          if wmax-wmin<1e-6: continue
          if g[p]>g[q]:
             sc=(g[p]-g[q])*wmax
          else:
             sc=(g[q]-g[p])*(-wmin)
          if sc>1e-9: P.append((p,q,float(wmin),float(wmax),float(sc)))
    act=np.where((h>1e-5)&(h<1-1e-5))[0]
    if act.size>1:
       for _ in range(20):
          p=int(rng.choice(act)); q=int(rng.choice(act))
          if p==q: continue
          hp=h[p]; hq=h[q]; wmin=hp-1.0; t=-hq
          if t>wmin: wmin=t
          wmax=hp; t=1-hq
          if t<wmax: wmax=t
          if wmax-wmin>1e-6:
             sc=abs(g[p]-g[q])*max(-wmin,wmax)+1e-8
             P.append((p,q,float(wmin),float(wmax),float(sc)))
    if len(P)>cap:
       scs=np.empty(len(P))
       for i in range(len(P)): scs[i]=P[i][4]
       ord2=np.argsort(scs)[::-1][:cap]
       R=[]
       for i in ord2: R.append(P[int(i)])
       return R
    return P
```
This is concise.

`top_weights` compact.

`pair_objective_pre` compact. Need no `n`? idxp/idxq/idx0. Good.
```
def pair_objective_pre(w,c,i1,i2,i3,i4,a1,a2,hp,hq,o0,op,oq,ip,ix,i0,lo,hi):
    if abs(w)<1e-15: return float(c.max())
    if w<lo or w>hi: return 1e100
    cn=c.copy()
    cn[i1]-=w*a1; cn[i2]+=w*a2; cn[i3]+=w*a1; cn[i4]-=w*a2
    pn=hp-w; qn=hq+w
    cn[i0]+=pn*(1-pn)+qn*(1-qn)-o0
    cn[ip]+=pn*(1-qn)-op
    cn[ix]+=qn*(1-pn)-oq
    m=float(cn.max()); return m if np.isfinite(m) else 1e100
```
Bounds tolerated? If optimizer calls slightly outside, return inf; could cause issues; use `if w<lo: w=lo; if w>hi: w=hi` before inf for small. Better:
```
if w<lo: w=lo
elif w>hi: w=hi
```
No inf. Since bounds ensure. Good. But if due bounds outside large, clamping may allow infeasible; okay.

`best_pair_value` compact. Need `maxiter=20` maybe. Use `xatol=1e-6`.
```
def best_pair_value(h,c,p,q,n):
    hp=h[p]; hq=h[q]; lo=max(hp-1.0,-hq); hi=min(hp,1-hq)
    if hi-lo<1e-7: return None
    v=np.arange(n); m=(v!=p)&(v!=q); vv=v[m]; hv=h[vv]
    i1=n-1+p-vv; i2=n-1+vv-p; i3=n-1+q-vv; i4=n-1+vv-q
    a1=1-hv; a2=hv; o0=hp*(1-hp)+hq*(1-hq); op=hp*(1-hq); oq=hq*(1-hp)
    try:
       r=minimize_scalar(pair_objective_pre,args=(c,i1,i2,i3,i4,a1,a2,hp,hq,o0,op,oq,n-1+p-q,n-1+q-p,n-1,lo,hi),bounds=(lo,hi),method='bounded',options={'maxiter':20,'xatol':1e-6})
    except Exception: return None
    if r is None or not np.isfinite(r.fun): return None
    return float(r.x), float(r.fun)
```
Good.

def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
    t0=time.time(); dl=t0+budget_s-3.0; rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    best_h=None; best_c=1e100
    try:
       par=np.array(initial_h_values,float)
    except Exception:
       par=None
    npar=300; par2=None
    if par is not None and par.size>0:
       npar=int(par.size)
       if npar%2: par2=resample(par,300); npar=300
       else: par2=project_box_sum(par,npar)
    else:
       par2=project_box_sum(np.full(300,0.5),300); npar=300
    # main
    end=t0+min(380.0,0.36*(dl-t0))
    h,c=pair_search(par2,npar,end,rng)
    if c<best_c: best_c=c; best_h=h
    # binary
    rem=dl-time.time()
    if rem>110:
       end=time.time()+min(70.0,0.09*rem)
       h,c=pair_search(binary_from(par2),npar,end,rng)
       if c<best_c: best_c=c; best_h=h
    # blocks
    for nb in (6,10):
       rem=dl-time.time()
       if rem<80: break
       end=time.time()+min(40.0,0.08*rem)
       h,c=pair_search(block_start(npar,rng,nb),npar,end,rng)
       if c<best_c: best_c=c; best_h=h
    # other n
    for nt,base in ((150,60),(200,70),(450,140)):
       rem=dl-time.time()
       if rem<90: break
       if best_h is not None and nt==len(best_h): continue
       hs=resample(best_h,nt) if best_h is not None else block_start(nt,rng,8)
       end=time.time()+min(base,0.25*rem)
       if end-time.time()<30: continue
       h,c=pair_search(hs,int(nt),end,rng)
       if c<best_c: best_c=c; best_h=h
    # final polish
    if best_h is not None and dl-time.time()>15:
       h,c=pair_search(best_h,len(best_h),min(dl-0.5,time.time()+min(50.0,0.5*(dl-time.time()))),rng,cap=10)
       if c<best_c: best_c=c; best_h=h
    if best_h is None:
       best_h=project_box_sum(np.full(300,0.5),300)
    best_h=project_box_sum(best_h,len(best_h))
    best_c=compute_c5(best_h)
    return best_h,best_c,len(best_h)
```

h_proj=project_box_sum(best_h,n)
c_proj=compute_c5(h_proj)
if c_proj < best_c: best_h,best_c=h_proj,c_proj
else: # still ensure feasibility? best_h likely feasible, but return best_h; maybe its sum slight off.
   if abs(sum - target)>1e-9: best_h=h_proj
```
But code complexity. To be safe, always project and accept c_proj. The change should be tiny (<1e-9). It might exceed target by 1e-7? If pre_c 0.3807, projection adding 1e-7 still below 0.3808. Good. Previous record 0.38085; final likely lower. We'll just project/recompute.

Maybe pair_search's best_h already projected; final project redundant but okay.

Now, if c_proj worse than best_c due to projection, best_c updates to c_proj (slightly higher) but still maybe target. Could store `pre_h` and if c_proj > best_c + 1e-12, revert to pre_h. But pre_h may not satisfy exact sum? Projected earlier. Let's implement simple:
```
    hproj=project_box_sum(best_h,len(best_h)); cproj=compute_c5(hproj)
    if cproj > best_c and abs(cproj-best_c) < 1e-8:
        # keep hproj? Need feasible. hproj feasible. best_c maybe not corresponding to hproj.
        best_h=hproj; best_c=cproj
    else:
        best_h=hproj; best_c=min(best_c,cproj)?
```
If we set best_c=min(best_c,cproj) but hproj c_proj > best_c, inconsistent. Better always project and set c. If projection worsens significantly, bug. It won't.

Now, output. Let's final code. Ensure `minimize_scalar` import. Use `np` and `time`.

Let's re-read pair_objective_pre: We update cn[ip] and cn[ix]; if ip or ix equals i0? Since p≠q, no. If ip/ix equals some i1/...? Could overlap if p-v = p-q => v=q excluded. v-p = p-q => v=2p-q maybe not excluded, could overlap between different indices arrays (e.g., i1 for some v and ip? Wait ip is p-q. For i1 = p-v. Equals p-q if v=q excluded. For i2 = v-p = p-q => v=2p-q. This index could coincide with ip? But ip is p-q, i2 could equal that if 2p-q=p-q => p=0? Let's see values: i2 offset = v-p; ip offset = p-q. Equality v-p = p-q -> v=2p-q. If v not p/q, possible. Then `cn[i2] +=` and `cn[ip] +=` both to same index; performing separate statements is fine (adds both). No within-same duplicate. Good.

Now, could `idx1` and `idx2` overlap causing two separate statements same index, fine.

Now, final answer only code fence. Ensure no extra text. Need final. Proceed.
</think>

```python
import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize_scalar

def full_corr(x, n):
    return np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode='full')

def compute_c5(h):
    n = len(h)
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')) * (2.0 / n))

def project_box_sum(a, n):
    a = np.clip(np.asarray(a, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0).copy()
    target = 0.5 * n
    lo = -float(a.max()) - 1.0
    hi = 1.0 - float(a.min()) + 1.0
    for _ in range(55):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = float(np.sum(np.clip(a + mid, 0.0, 1.0)))
        if s < target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    x = np.clip(a + 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - float(x.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-13:
        idx = np.where((x > 1e-12) & (x < 1.0 - 1e-12))[0]
        if idx.size > 0:
            x[idx] += diff / idx.size
        else:
            i = int(np.argmax(1.0 - x)) if diff > 0 else int(np.argmax(x))
            i[-1] += diff if False else x[i] = x[i] + diff
        x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    return x

def binary_from(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = len(h)
    out = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    idx = np.argsort(h)[::-1]
    out[idx[:n // 2]] = 1.0
    return project_box_sum(out, n)

def resample(h, n):
    if len(h) == n:
        return project_box_sum(h, n)
    x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, len(h))
    x2 = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n)
    return project_box_sum(np.interp(x2, x1, h), n)

def block_start(n, rng, nblocks):
    wf = rng.dirichlet(np.ones(nblocks))
    lens = np.floor(n * wf).astype(int)
    rem = n - int(lens.sum())
    for i in range(rem):
        lens[i % len(lens)] += 1
    lens = lens[lens > 0]
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    pos = 0
    val = float(rng.randint(0, 2))
    for L in lens:
        L = int(L)
        if L <= 0 or pos >= n:
            continue
        if pos + L > n:
            L = n - pos
        h[pos:pos + L] = val
        pos += L
        val = 1.0 - val
    if pos < n:
        h[pos:] = val
    target = n // 2
    cur = int(round(float(h.sum())))
    diff = cur - target
    if diff > 0:
        ones = np.where(h > 0.5)[0]
        k = min(diff, ones.size)
        if k > 0:
            h[rng.choice(ones, k, replace=False)] = 0.0
    elif diff < 0:
        zeros = np.where(h <= 0.5)[0]
        k = min(-diff, zeros.size)
        if k > 0:
            h[rng.choice(zeros, k, replace=False)] = 1.0
    return project_box_sum(h, n)

def top_weights(c, top_k):
    if len(c) > top_k:
        idx = np.argsort(c)[-top_k:]
    else:
        idx = np.arange(len(c))
    cc = c[idx]
    m = float(cc.max())
    sh = 40.0 * (cc - m)
    sh = sh - float(sh.max())
    w = np.exp(sh)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        w = np.ones_like(w) / w.size
    else:
        w = w / s
    return idx, w

def grad_from_idx(h, n, idx, weights):
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for ii in range(idx.size):
        w = float(weights[ii])
        if w <= 1e-15:
            continue
        k = int(idx[ii])
        d = k - (n - 1)
        if d == 0:
            g += w * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)
        elif d > 0:
            m = d
            g[:n - m] -= w * h[m:]
            g[m:] += w * (1.0 - h[:n - m])
        else:
            m = -d
            g[:n - m] += w * (1.0 - h[m:])
            g[m:] -= w * h[:n - m]
    return g

def pair_objective_pre(w, c, i1, i2, i3, i4, a1, a2, hp, hq, o0, op, oq, ip, iq, i0, lo, hi):
    if not np.isfinite(w):
        return 1e100
    if w < lo:
        w = lo
    elif w > hi:
        w = hi
    if abs(w) < 1e-15:
        return float(c.max())
    cn = c.copy()
    cn[i1] -= w * a1
    cn[i2] += w * a2
    cn[i3] += w * a1
    cn[i4] -= w * a2
    pn = hp - w
    qn = hq + w
    cn[i0] += pn * (1.0 - pn) + qn * (1.0 - qn) - o0
    cn[ip] += pn * (1.0 - qn) - op
    cn[iq] += qn * (1.0 - pn) - oq
    m = float(cn.max())
    return m if np.isfinite(m) else 1e100

def best_pair_value(h, c, p, q, n):
    hp = float(h[p])
    hq = float(h[q])
    lo = max(hp - 1.0, -hq)
    hi = min(hp, 1.0 - hq)
    if hi - lo < 1e-7:
        return None
    v = np.arange(n)
    mask = (v != p) & (v != q)
    vv = v[mask]
    hv = h[vv]
    i1 = n - 1 + (p - vv)
    i2 = n - 1 + (vv - p)
    i3 = n - 1 + (q - vv)
    i4 = n - 1 + (vv - q)
    a1 = 1.0 - hv
    a2 = hv
    o0 = hp * (1.0 - hp) + hq * (1.0 - hq)
    op = hp * (1.0 - hq)
    oq = hq * (1.0 - hp)
    try:
        r = minimize_scalar(
            pair_objective_pre,
            args=(c, i1, i2, i3, i4, a1, a2, hp, hq, o0, op, oq,
                   n - 1 + p - q, n - 1 + q - p, n - 1, lo, hi),
            bounds=(lo, hi), method='bounded',
            options={'maxiter': 20, 'xatol': 1e-6}
        )
    except Exception:
        return None
    if r is None or not np.isfinite(r.fun):
        return None
    return float(r.x), float(r.fun)

def make_pairs(h, g, n, rng, cap):
    P = []
    order = np.argsort(g)
    low = order[:30]
    high = order[-30:]
    for p in high:
        p = int(p)
        hp = float(h[p])
        if hp <= 1e-7:
            continue
        gp = float(g[p])
        for q in low:
            q = int(q)
            if p == q:
                continue
            hq = float(h[q])
            wmin = hp - 1.0
            t = -hq
            if t > wmin:
                wmin = t
            wmax = hp
            t = 1.0 - hq
            if t < wmax:
                wmax = t
            if wmax - wmin < 1e-6:
                continue
            if gp > float(g[q]):
                sc = (gp - float(g[q])) * wmax
            else:
                sc = (float(g[q]) - gp) * (-wmin)
            if sc > 1e-9:
                P.append((p, q, float(wmin), float(wmax), float(sc)))
    act = np.where((h > 1e-5) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-5))[0]
    if act.size > 1:
        for _ in range(20):
            p = int(rng.choice(act))
            q = int(rng.choice(act))
            if p == q:
                continue
            hp = float(h[p])
            hq = float(h[q])
            wmin = hp - 1.0
            t = -hq
            if t > wmin:
                wmin = t
            wmax = hp
            t = 1.0 - hq
            if t < wmax:
                wmax = t
            if wmax - wmin > 1e-6:
                sc = abs(float(g[p]) - float(g[q])) * max(-wmin, wmax) + 1e-8
                P.append((p, q, float(wmin), float(wmax), float(sc)))
    if len(P) > cap:
        scs = np.empty(len(P), dtype=float)
        for i in range(len(P)):
            scs[i] = P[i][4]
        ord2 = np.argsort(scs)[::-1][:cap]
        R = []
        for i in ord2:
            R.append(P[int(i)])
        return R
    return P

def pair_search(h0, n, end_time, rng, cap=None):
    if cap is None:
        if n <= 200:
            cap = 48
        elif n <= 350:
            cap = 36
        else:
            cap = 20
    h = project_box_sum(h0, n)
    c = full_corr(h, n)
    cur = float(c.max())
    best = h.copy()
    best_raw = cur
    no = 0
    while time.time() < end_time - 1.0:
        cur = float(c.max())
        if cur < best_raw:
            best_raw = cur
            best = h.copy()
        idx, w = top_weights(c, 32)
        g = grad_from_idx(h, n, idx, w)
        pairs = make_pairs(h, g, n, rng, max(30, cap * 2))
        improved = False
        for p, q, wmin, wmax, sc in pairs:
            if time.time() > end_time - 1.5:
                break
            mv = best_pair_value(h, c, p, q, n)
            if mv is None:
                continue
            ww, val = mv
            if val < cur - 1e-9:
                h2 = h.copy()
                h2[p] = float(np.clip(h[p] - ww, 0.0, 1.0))
                h2[q] = float(np.clip(h[q] + ww, 0.0, 1.0))
                h2 = project_box_sum(h2, n)
                c2 = full_corr(h2, n)
                nc = float(c2.max())
                if nc < cur - 1e-12:
                    h = h2
                    c = c2
                    improved = True
                    no = 0
                    break
        if not improved:
            no += 1
            if no >= 25:
                if time.time() < end_time - 30:
                    scale = min(0.25, 0.006 + 0.0035 * float(no))
                    h = project_box_sum(best + rng.normal(0.0, scale, n), n)
                    c = full_corr(h, n)
                    no = 0
                else:
                    break
    return best, float(full_corr(best, n).max()) * (2.0 / n)

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + budget_s - 3.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    best_h = None
    best_c = 1e100

    try:
        par = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
    except Exception:
        par = None

    if par is not None and par.size > 0:
        npar = int(par.size)
        if npar % 2 == 1:
            par = resample(par, 300)
            npar = 300
        else:
            par = project_box_sum(par, npar)
    else:
        npar = 300
        par = project_box_sum(np.full(npar, 0.5), npar)

    end = t0 + min(450.0, 0.45 * (deadline - t0))
    h, c = pair_search(par, npar, end, rng)
    if c < best_c:
        best_c = c
        best_h = h

    rem = deadline - time.time()
    if rem > 110:
        end = time.time() + min(70.0, 0.09 * rem)
        h, c = pair_search(binary_from(par), npar, end, rng)
        if c < best_c:
            best_c = c
            best_h = h

    for nb in (6, 10):
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        if rem < 80:
            break
        end = time.time() + min(40.0, 0.08 * rem)
        h, c = pair_search(block_start(npar, rng, nb), npar, end, rng)
        if c < best_c:
            best_c = c
            best_h = h

    for nt, base in ((150, 60), (200, 80), (450, 160), (600, 150)):
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        if rem < 90:
            break
        if best_h is not None and nt == len(best_h):
            continue
        if best_h is not None:
            hs = resample(best_h, nt)
        else:
            hs = block_start(nt, rng, 8)
        end = time.time() + min(base, 0.25 * rem)
        if end - time.time() < 30:
            continue
        h, c = pair_search(hs, int(nt), end, rng)
        if c < best_c:
            best_c = c
            best_h = h

    if best_h is not None and deadline - time.time() > 15:
        end = min(deadline - 0.5, time.time() + min(50.0, 0.5 * (deadline - time.time())))
        h, c = pair_search(best_h, len(best_h), end, rng, cap=10)
        if c < best_c:
            best_c = c
            best_h = h

    if best_h is None:
        best_h = project_box_sum(np.full(300, 0.5), 300)

    best_h = project_box_sum(best_h, len(best_h))
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h)
    return best_h, best_c, len(best_h)
```
